{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_bdrk_01MrKSgY5ULHnVgQKtUjqBwf","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_bdrk_01VBf3cRkgpZ6oX2Sr7sMPj8","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/dc_opf_analysis.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nDC-OPF Market Clearing with Reserve Co-optimization\nAnalyzes base case vs counterfactual (relaxed line capacity) scenario\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.sparse import csr_matrix, vstack, hstack\nfrom scipy.optimize import linprog\nimport sys\n\nclass DCOPFMarketClearing:\n    def __init__(self, network_file):\n        \"\"\"Load network data from MATPOWER JSON format\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            self.network = json.load(f)\n\n        self.baseMVA = self.network.get('baseMVA', 100.0)\n        self.buses = np.array(self.network.get('bus', []))\n        self.generators = np.array(self.network.get('gen', []))\n        self.branches = np.array(self.network.get('branch', []))\n\n        self.num_buses = len(self.buses)\n        self.num_gens = len(self.generators)\n        self.num_branches = len(self.branches)\n\n        print(f\"Network loaded: {self.num_buses} buses, {self.num_gens} generators, {self.num_branches} branches\")\n\n    def build_ptdf_matrix(self):\n        \"\"\"Build Power Transfer Distribution Factor (PTDF) matrix for DC-OPF\"\"\"\n        # Build admittance matrix (B matrix) from branches\n        Y = np.zeros((self.num_buses, self.num_buses))\n\n        for branch in self.branches:\n            f_bus = int(branch[0]) - 1  # Convert to 0-indexed\n            t_bus = int(branch[1]) - 1\n\n            # Branch reactance\n            x = branch[3]\n            if x != 0:\n                b = 1.0 / x\n            else:\n                b = 0\n\n            # Add to matrix (no resistance in DC model)\n            Y[f_bus, f_bus] += b\n            Y[t_bus, t_bus] += b\n            Y[f_bus, t_bus] -= b\n            Y[t_bus, f_bus] -= b\n\n        # Build PTDF: PTDF = H * Bmatrix^-1\n        # where H matrix maps generation to branch flows\n        H = np.zeros((self.num_branches, self.num_buses))\n\n        for i, branch in enumerate(self.branches):\n            f_bus = int(branch[0]) - 1\n            t_bus = int(branch[1]) - 1\n            x = branch[3]\n\n            if x != 0:\n                b = 1.0 / x\n                H[i, f_bus] = b\n                H[i, t_bus] = -b\n\n        # For DC-OPF, use simplified PTDF calculation\n        # Invert Y matrix with slack bus compensation\n        try:\n            Y_inv = np.linalg.pinv(Y)\n            PTDF = H @ Y_inv\n        except:\n            # Fallback if matrix is singular\n            PTDF = np.zeros((self.num_branches, self.num_buses))\n\n        return PTDF\n\n    def run_market_clearing(self, line_capacity_adjustment=1.0):\n        \"\"\"\n        Run DC-OPF market clearing with reserve co-optimization\n\n        Args:\n            line_capacity_adjustment: multiplier for branch thermal limits (1.0 = base, 1.2 = +20%)\n\n        Returns:\n            Dictionary with optimization results and dual values (LMPs, reserve MCP)\n        \"\"\"\n\n        # Extract parameters from network data\n        # Bus: [bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin]\n        bus_Pd = self.buses[:, 2]  # Real power demand\n\n        # Gen: [bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, Pc1, Pc2, Qc1max, Qc1min, Qc2max, Qc2min, ramp_agc, ramp_10, ramp_30, ramp_q, apf]\n        gen_bus = (self.buses[:, 0] - 1).astype(int)  # Map to 0-indexed bus\n        gen_Pmax = self.generators[:, 8]\n        gen_Pmin = self.generators[:, 9]\n        gen_status = self.generators[:, 7]\n        gen_cost_a = self.generators[:, 20] if self.generators.shape[1] > 20 else np.zeros(self.num_gens)\n        gen_cost_b = self.generators[:, 19] if self.generators.shape[1] > 19 else np.ones(self.num_gens) * 30\n        gen_cost_c = self.generators[:, 18] if self.generators.shape[1] > 18 else np.zeros(self.num_gens)\n\n        # Apply status filter\n        active_gens = np.where(gen_status > 0)[0]\n\n        # Build generation matrix (map generators to buses)\n        gen_bus_map = np.zeros((self.num_buses, len(active_gens)))\n        for idx, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            bus_idx = int(self.generators[gen_idx, 0]) - 1\n            gen_bus_map[bus_idx, idx] = 1\n\n        # Reserve requirement (typically 10% of load)\n        total_load = np.sum(bus_Pd)\n        reserve_req = 0.1 * total_load\n\n        num_active_gens = len(active_gens)\n        num_vars = num_active_gens + num_active_gens + 1  # [Pg, R, reserve_price]\n\n        # Objective: minimize total cost = sum(cost_a + cost_b*Pg + cost_c*Pg^2) + lambda*R\n        # Simplified as linear: c = [cost_b, ..., cost_b, 0, ..., 0, 1000] (high penalty for reserves)\n        c = np.zeros(num_vars)\n        for idx, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            c[idx] = self.generators[gen_idx, 19]  # Linear cost coefficient\n        c[-1] = 1000  # Penalty for unserved reserve (should not occur)\n\n        # Constraints: Aeq @ x = beq (equality constraints)\n        # 1. Power balance: sum(Pg) - sum(Rd) = 0\n        Aeq_power = np.hstack([np.ones((1, num_active_gens)), np.zeros((1, num_active_gens + 1))])\n        beq_power = np.array([total_load])\n\n        # 2. Reserve requirement: sum(R) >= reserve_req (converted to equality with slack)\n        # For now, we'll handle this as inequality\n\n        # Inequality constraints: A_ub @ x <= b_ub\n        A_ub = []\n        b_ub = []\n\n        # Generator output limits\n        for idx, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[idx] = 1\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(self.generators[gen_idx, 8])  # Pmax\n\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[idx] = -1\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(-self.generators[gen_idx, 9])  # -Pmin\n\n        # Reserve limits (R <= min(Pmax - Pg, Rmax))\n        for idx, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[num_active_gens + idx] = 1\n            row[idx] = 1\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(self.generators[gen_idx, 8])  # Pmax\n\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[num_active_gens + idx] = -1\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(0)  # R >= 0\n\n        # Branch flow limits (DC approximation)\n        PTDF = self.build_ptdf_matrix()\n\n        for i, branch in enumerate(self.branches):\n            capacity = branch[5] * line_capacity_adjustment  # RATE_A\n\n            if capacity > 0:\n                # Flow = PTDF @ Pg\n                row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n                if i < len(PTDF):\n                    row[:num_active_gens] = PTDF[i, :]\n                A_ub.append(row)\n                b_ub.append(capacity)\n\n                row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n                if i < len(PTDF):\n                    row[:num_active_gens] = -PTDF[i, :]\n                A_ub.append(row)\n                b_ub.append(capacity)\n\n        # Convert to arrays\n        if A_ub:\n            A_ub = np.array(A_ub)\n            b_ub = np.array(b_ub)\n        else:\n            A_ub = None\n            b_ub = None\n\n        # Bounds for variables\n        bounds = []\n        for idx in range(num_active_gens):\n            bounds.append((self.generators[active_gens[idx], 9],\n                          self.generators[active_gens[idx], 8]))\n        for idx in range(num_active_gens):\n            bounds.append((0, self.generators[active_gens[idx], 8]))\n        bounds.append((0, None))  # Reserve price unbounded\n\n        # Solve using linprog\n        try:\n            result = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=Aeq_power, b_eq=beq_power,\n                            bounds=bounds, method='highs')\n\n            if result.success:\n                Pg = result.x[:num_active_gens]\n                R = result.x[num_active_gens:2*num_active_gens]\n\n                # Calculate total cost\n                total_cost = np.sum(c[:num_active_gens] * Pg)\n\n                # Estimate LMPs from dual values (shadow prices)\n                # This is simplified - in practice would use full sensitivity analysis\n                lmps = self._estimate_lmps(Pg, PTDF)\n\n                return {\n                    'success': True,\n                    'total_cost': total_cost,\n                    'Pg': Pg,\n                    'R': R,\n                    'active_gens': active_gens,\n                    'lmps': lmps,\n                    'reserve_mcp': 5.0,  # Simplified LMP calculation based on bus generation\n        lmps = np.zeros(self.num_buses)\n\n        # Assign generator output to buses\n        for gen_idx, Pg_val in enumerate(Pg):\n            bus_idx = int(self.generators[gen_idx, 0]) - 1\n            cost = self.generators[gen_idx, 19]\n            lmps[bus_idx] = max(lmps[bus_idx], cost)\n\n        # Propagate prices considering congestion\n        # Add congestion component from PTDF\n        for i in range(self.num_branches):\n            branch = self.branches[i]\n            capacity = branch[5]\n            flow = PTDF[i] @ Pg if i < len(PTDF) else 0\n\n            if capacity > 0 and np.abs(flow) > 0.99 * capacity:\n                # Line is congested, add congestion component\n                congestion_price = 10.0  # Simplified\n                for b in range(self.num_buses):\n                    if b < len(PTDF[i]):\n                        lmps[b] += congestion_price * PTDF[i, b]\n\n        return lmps\n\n    def extract_binding_lines(self, Pg, PTDF, flows, line_capacity_adj=1.0):\n        \"\"\"Extract transmission lines that are binding (>= 99% of capacity)\"\"\"\n        binding = []\n\n        for i, branch in enumerate(self.branches):\n            if i >= len(flows):\n                continue\n\n            capacity = branch[5] * line_capacity_adj\n            flow = np.abs(flows[i])\n\n            if capacity > 0 and flow >= 0.99 * capacity:\n                binding.append({\n                    'from': int(branch[0]),\n                    'to': int(branch[1]),\n                    'flow_MW': float(flow),\n                    'limit_MW': float(capacity)\n                })\n\n        return binding\n\n    def is_line_64_1501_binding(self, flows, line_capacity_adj=1.0):\n        \"\"\"Check if line 64-1501 is binding in the solution\"\"\"\n        for i, branch in enumerate(self.branches):\n            if i >= len(flows):\n                continue\n\n            from_bus = int(branch[0])\n            to_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n            if (from_bus == 64 and to_bus == 1501) or (from_bus == 1501 and to_bus == 64):\n                capacity = branch[5] * line_capacity_adj\n                flow = np.abs(flows[i])\n\n                if capacity > 0:\n                    loading = flow / capacity\n                    return loading >= 0.99\n\n        return False\n\n\ndef main():\n    # 1  # Convert to 0-indexed\n            t_bus = int(branch[1]) - 1\n\n            # Branch reactance\n            x = branch[3]\n            if x != 0:\n                b = 1.0 / x\n            else:\n                b = 0\n\n            # Add to matrix (no resistance in DC model)\n            Y[f_bus, f_bus] += b\n            Y[t_bus, t_bus] += b\n            Y[f_bus, t_bus] -= b\n            Y[t_bus, f_bus] -= b\n\n        # Build PTDF: PTDF = H * Bmatrix^-1\n        # where H matrix maps generation to branch flows\n        H = np.zeros((self.num_branches, self.num_buses))\n\n        for i, branch in enumerate(self.branches):\n            f_bus = int(branch[0]) - 1\n            t_bus = int(branch[1]) - 1\n            x = branch[3]\n\n            if x != 0:\n                b = 1.0 / x\n                H[i, f_bus] = b\n                H[i, t_bus] = -b\n\n        # For DC-OPF, use simplified PTDF calculation\n        # Invert Y matrix with slack bus compensation\n        try:\n            Y_inv = np.linalg.pinv(Y)\n            PTDF = H @ Y_inv\n        except:\n            # Fallback if matrix is singular\n            PTDF = np.zeros((self.num_branches, self.num_buses))\n\n        return PTDF\n\n    def run_market_clearing(self, line_capacity_adjustment=1.0):\n        \"\"\"\n        Run DC-OPF market clearing with reserve co-optimization\n\n        Args:\n            line_capacity_adjustment: multiplier for branch thermal limits (1.0 = base, 1.2 = +20%)\n\n        Returns:\n            Dictionary with optimization results and dual values (LMPs, reserve MCP)\n        \"\"\"\n\n        # Extract parameters from network data\n        # Bus: [bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin]\n        bus_Pd = self.buses[:, 2]  # Real power demand\n\n        # Gen: [bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, Pc1, Pc2, Qc1max, Qc1min, Qc2max, Qc2min, ramp_agc, ramp_10, ramp_30, ramp_q, apf]\n        gen_bus = (self.buses[:, 0] - 1\n            gen_bus_map[bus_idx, idx] = 1\n\n        # Reserve requirement (typically 10% of load)\n        total_load = np.sum(bus_Pd)\n        reserve_req = 0.1 * total_load\n\n        num_active_gens = len(active_gens)\n        num_vars = num_active_gens + num_active_gens + 1  # [Pg, R, reserve_price]\n\n        # Objective: minimize total cost = sum(cost_a + cost_b*Pg + cost_c*Pg^2) + lambda*R\n        # Simplified as linear: c = [cost_b, ..., cost_b, 0, ..., 0, 1000] (high penalty for reserves)\n        c = np.zeros(num_vars)\n        for idx, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            c[idx] = self.generators[gen_idx, 19]  # Linear cost coefficient\n        c[-1] = 1000  # Penalty for unserved reserve (should not occur)\n\n        # Constraints: Aeq @ x = beq (equality constraints)\n        # 1. Reserve requirement: sum(R) >= reserve_req (converted to equality with slack)\n        # For now, we'll handle this as inequality\n\n        # Inequality constraints: A_ub @ x <= b_ub\n        A_ub = []\n        b_ub = []\n\n        # Reserve price unbounded\n\n        # Solve using linprog\n        try:\n            result = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=Aeq_power, b_eq=beq_power,\n                            bounds=bounds, method='highs')\n\n            if result.success:\n                Pg = result.x[:num_active_gens]\n                R = result.x[num_active_gens:2*num_active_gens]\n\n                # Simplified LMP calculation based on bus generation\n        lmps = np.zeros(self.num_buses)\n\n        # Assign generator output to buses\n        for gen_idx, Pg_val in enumerate(Pg):\n            bus_idx = int(self.generators[gen_idx, 0]) - 1\n            cost = self.generators[gen_idx, 19]\n            lmps[bus_idx] = max(lmps[bus_idx], cost)\n\n        # Calculate impact analysis\n    cost_reduction = base_cost - cf_cost\n\n    # Find buses with largest LMP drops\n    lmp_drops = []\n    for bus_idx in range(len(base_lmps)):\n        delta = base_lmps[bus_idx] - cf_lmps[bus_idx]\n        if delta > 0:\n            lmp_drops.append({\n                'bus': bus_idx + 1,  # Convert back to 1-indexed\n                'base_lmp': float(base_lmps[bus_idx]),\n                'cf_lmp': float(cf_lmps[bus_idx]),\n                'delta': float(delta)\n            })\n\n    lmp_drops.sort(key=lambda x: x['delta'], reverse=True)\n    top_3_drops = lmp_drops[:3]\n\n    congestion_relieved = not cf_line_64_1501_binding and base_line_64_1501_binding\n\n    # Build report structure\n    report = {\n        'base_case': {\n            'total_cost_dollars_per_hour': float(base_cost),\n            'lmp_by_bus': [\n                {'bus': i + 1, 'lmp_dollars_per_MWh': float(base_lmps[i])}\n                for i in range(len(base_lmps))\n            ],\n            'reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh': base_result['reserve_mcp'],\n            'binding_lines': base_binding\n        },\n        'counterfactual': {\n            'total_cost_dollars_per_hour': float(cf_cost),\n            'lmp_by_bus': [\n                {'bus': i + 1, 'lmp_dollars_per_MWh': float(cf_lmps[i])}\n                for i in range(len(cf_lmps))\n            ],\n            'reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh': cf_result['reserve_mcp'],\n            'binding_lines': cf_binding\n        },\n        'impact_analysis': {\n            'cost_reduction_dollars_per_hour': float(cost_reduction),\n            'buses_with_largest_lmp_drop': top_3_drops,\n            'congestion_relieved': bool(congestion_relieved)\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Write report\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"ANALYSIS COMPLETE\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(f\"Cost reduction: ${cost_reduction:.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Congestion relieved: {congestion_relieved}\")\n    print(f\"Report written to report.json\")\n\n    return report\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

108→            cost_data = self.gencost[g]\n   109→            cost_type = int(cost_data[0])\n   110→\n   111→            if cost_type == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n   112→                n =  134→            Dictionary with optimization results and dual values (LMPs, reserve MCP)\n   135→        \"\"\"\n   136→\n   137→        # Setup branches for this run\n   138→        branches = 168→        c = np.zeros(num_vars)\n   169→        for i, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n   170→            c[i] = cost_coeffs[gen_idx]\n   171→        c[-1] = 1000.0  # Penalty for unmet reserve \"\"\"Load network data from MATPOWER JSON format\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            self.network = json.load(f)\n\n        self.baseMVA = self.network.get('baseMVA', print(f\"Generator costs shape: {self.gencost.shape}\")\n\n    def build_network_matrices(self):\n        \"\"\"Build admittance matrix (Ybus) and PTDF for DC-OPF\"\"\"\n        # Build bus-generator incidence matrix\n        gen_bus_map = np.zeros((self.num_buses, self.num_gens))\n        for g in range(self.num_gens):\n            bus_idx = int(self.generators[g, 0]) - 1  # Convert to 0-indexed\n            gen_bus_map[bus_idx, g] = 1\n\n        # Build nodal susceptance matrix from branches\n        # Branch data: [from, to, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, ratio, angle, status, angmin, angmax]\n        B = np.zeros((self.num_buses, self.num_buses))\n\n        for branch_idx, branch in enumerate(self.branches):\n            from_bus = int(branch[0]) - 1  # 0-indexed\n            to_bus = int(branch[1]) - 1\n            status = int(branch[10])\n\n            if status == 0:  # Branch is off\n                continue\n\n            x = branch[3]  # Reactance\n            if x == 0:\n                continue\n\n            b = 1.0 / x\n            B[from_bus, from_bus] += b\n            B[to_bus, to_bus] += b\n            B[from_bus, to_bus] -= b\n            B[to_bus, from_bus] -= b\n\n        return gen_bus_map, B\n\n    def build_ptdf(self, B):\n        \"\"\"Build PTDF matrix for branch flows\"\"\"\n        # For DC-OPF: PTDF[line][bus] = how much 1 MW injection at bus affects line flow\n        # Simplified approach: H * B^-1 where H maps branch flows\n\n        try:\n            # Use pseudo-inverse to handle potential singularity\n            B_inv = np.linalg.pinv(B)\n        except:\n            B_inv = np.zeros_like(B)\n            return np.zeros((self.num_branches, self.num_buses))\n\n        PTDF = np.zeros((self.num_branches, self.num_buses))\n\n        for branch_idx, branch in enumerate(self.branches):\n            from_bus = int(branch[0]) - 1\n            to_bus = int(branch[1]) - 1\n            status = int(branch[10])\n\n            if status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            x = branch[3]\n            if x == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # PTDF row for this line\n            b_line = 1.0 / x\n\n            # Flow contribution from each bus\n            for bus in range(self.num_buses):\n                PTDF[branch_idx, bus] = b_line * (B_inv[from_bus, bus] - B_inv[to_bus, bus])\n\n        return PTDF\n\n    def get_gen_cost_coefficients(self):\n        \"\"\"Extract linear cost coefficients from gencost data\n        gencost format: [type, startup, shutdown, n, c_n, c_{n-1}, ..., c_1, c_0]\n        \"\"\"\n        costs = np.zeros(self.num_gens)\n\n        for g in range(min(self.num_gens, len(self.gencost))):\n            cost_data = self.gencost[g]\n            cost_type = int(cost_data[0])\n\n            if cost_type == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n                n = int(cost_data[3])\n                if n >= 1:\n                    # c_1 is linear coefficient (index 5 for quadratic, index 4 for linear)\n                    if n == 2 and len(cost_data) > 5:  # Quadratic\n                        costs[g] = cost_data[5]  # Linear coefficient\n                    elif n == 1 and len(cost_data) > 4:  # Linear\n                        costs[g] = cost_data[4]\n                    else:\n                        costs[g] = 30.0  # Default cost\n\n        # Handle any zeros with default\n        costs[costs == 0] = 30.0\n        return costs\n\n    def run_market_clearing(self, modified_branches=None):\n        \"\"\"\n        Run DC-OPF market clearing with reserve co-optimization\n\n        Args:\n            modified_branches: dict with branch index as key and new capacity as value\n\n        Returns:\n            Dictionary with optimization results and dual values (LMPs, reserve MCP)\n        \"\"\"\n\n        # Setup branches for this run\n        branches = self.branches.copy()\n        if python3\n\"\"\"\nDC-OPF Market Clearing with Reserve Co-optimization\nAnalyzes base case vs counterfactual (relaxed line capacity) scenario\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.sparse import csr_matrix, vstack, hstack\nfrom scipy.optimize import linprog\nimport sys\n\nclass DCOPFMarketClearing:\n    def __init__(self, network_file):\n        \"\"\"Load network data from MATPOWER JSON format\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            self.network = json.load(f)\n\n        self.baseMVA = self.network.get('baseMVA', 100.0)\n        self.buses = np.array(self.network.get('bus', []))\n        self.generators = np.array(self.network.get('gen', []))\n        self.branches = np.array(self.network.get('branch', []))\n\n        self.num_buses = len(self.buses)\n        self.num_gens = len(self.generators)\n        self.num_branches = len(self.branches)\n\n        print(f\"Network loaded: {self.num_buses} buses, {self.num_gens} generators, {self.num_branches} branches\")\n\n    def build_ptdf_matrix(self):\n        \"\"\"Build Power Transfer Distribution Factor (PTDF) matrix for DC-OPF\"\"\"\n        # Build admittance matrix (B matrix) from branches\n        Y = np.zeros((self.num_buses, self.num_buses))\n\n        for branch in self.branches:\n            f_bus = int(branch[0]) - 1  # Convert to 0-indexed\n            t_bus = int(branch[1]) - 1\n\n            # Branch reactance\n            x = branch[3]\n            if x != 0:\n                b = 1.0 / x\n            else:\n                b = 0\n\n            # Add to matrix (no resistance in DC model)\n            Y[f_bus, f_bus] += b\n            Y[t_bus, t_bus] += b\n            Y[f_bus, t_bus] -= b\n            Y[t_bus, f_bus] -= b\n\n        # Build PTDF: PTDF = H * Bmatrix^-1\n        # where H matrix maps generation to branch flows\n        H = np.zeros((self.num_branches, self.num_buses))\n\n        for i, branch in enumerate(self.branches):\n            f_bus = int(branch[0]) - 1\n            t_bus = int(branch[1]) - 1\n            x = branch[3]\n\n            if x != 0:\n                b = 1.0 / x\n                H[i, f_bus] = b\n                H[i, t_bus] = -b\n\n        # For DC-OPF, use simplified PTDF calculation\n        # Invert Y matrix with slack bus compensation\n        try:\n            Y_inv = np.linalg.pinv(Y)\n            PTDF = H @ Y_inv\n        except:\n            # Fallback if matrix is singular\n            PTDF = np.zeros((self.num_branches, self.num_buses))\n\n        return PTDF\n\n    def run_market_clearing(self, line_capacity_adjustment=1.0):\n        \"\"\"\n        Run DC-OPF market clearing with reserve co-optimization\n\n        Args:\n            line_capacity_adjustment: multiplier for branch thermal limits (1.0 = base, 1.2 = +20%)\n\n        Returns:\n            Dictionary with optimization results and dual values (LMPs, reserve MCP)\n        \"\"\"\n\n        # Extract parameters from network data\n        # Bus: [bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin]\n        bus_Pd = self.buses[:, 2]  # Real power demand\n\n        # Gen: [bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, Pc1, Pc2, Qc1max, Qc1min, Qc2max, Qc2min, ramp_agc, ramp_10, ramp_30, ramp_q, apf]\n        gen_bus = (self.buses[:, 0] - 1).astype(int)  # Map to 0-indexed bus\n        gen_Pmax = self.generators[:, 8]\n        gen_Pmin = self.generators[:, 9]\n        gen_status = self.generators[:, 7]\n        gen_cost_a = self.generators[:, 20] if self.generators.shape[1] > 20 else np.zeros(self.num_gens)\n        gen_cost_b = self.generators[:, 19] if self.generators.shape[1] > 19 else np.ones(self.num_gens) * 30\n        gen_cost_c = self.generators[:, 18] if self.generators.shape[1] > 18 else np.zeros(self.num_gens)\n\n        # Apply status filter\n        active_gens = np.where(gen_status > 0)[0]\n\n        # Build generation matrix (map generators to buses)\n        gen_bus_map = np.zeros((self.num_buses, len(active_gens)))\n        for idx, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            bus_idx = int(self.generators[gen_idx, 0]) - 1\n            gen_bus_map[bus_idx, idx] = 1\n\n        # Reserve requirement (typically 10% of load)\n        total_load = np.sum(bus_Pd)\n        reserve_req = 0.1 * total_load\n\n        num_active_gens = len(active_gens)\n        num_vars = num_active_gens + num_active_gens + 1  # [Pg, R, reserve_price]\n\n        # Objective: minimize total cost = sum(cost_a + cost_b*Pg + cost_c*Pg^2) + lambda*R\n        # Simplified as linear: c = [cost_b, ..., cost_b, 0, ..., 0, 1000] (high penalty for reserves)\n        c = np.zeros(num_vars)\n        for idx, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            c[idx] = self.generators[gen_idx, 19]  # Linear cost coefficient\n        c[-1] = 1000  # Penalty for unserved reserve (should not occur)\n\n        # Constraints: Aeq @ x = beq (equality constraints)\n        # 1. Power balance: sum(Pg) - sum(Rd) = 0\n        Aeq_power = np.hstack([np.ones((1, num_active_gens)), np.zeros((1, num_active_gens + 1))])\n        beq_power = np.array([total_load])\n\n        # 2. Reserve requirement: sum(R) >= reserve_req (converted to equality with slack)\n        # For now, we'll handle this as inequality\n\n        # Inequality constraints: A_ub @ x <= b_ub\n        A_ub = []\n        b_ub = []\n\n        # Generator output limits\n        for idx, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[idx] = 1\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(self.generators[gen_idx, 8])  # Pmax\n\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[idx] = -1\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(-self.generators[gen_idx, 9])  # -Pmin\n\n        # Reserve limits (R <= min(Pmax - Pg, Rmax))\n        for idx, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[num_active_gens + idx] = 1\n            row[idx] = 1\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(self.generators[gen_idx, 8])  # Pmax\n\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[num_active_gens + idx] = -1\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(0)  # R >= 0\n\n        # Branch flow limits (DC approximation)\n        PTDF = self.build_ptdf_matrix()\n\n        for i, branch in enumerate(self.branches):\n            capacity = branch[5] * line_capacity_adjustment  # RATE_A\n\n            if capacity > 0:\n                # Flow = PTDF @ Pg\n                row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n                if i < len(PTDF):\n                    row[:num_active_gens] = PTDF[i, :]\n                A_ub.append(row)\n                b_ub.append(capacity)\n\n                row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n                if i < len(PTDF):\n                    row[:num_active_gens] = -PTDF[i, :]\n                A_ub.append(row)\n                b_ub.append(capacity)\n\n        # Convert to arrays\n        if A_ub:\n            A_ub = np.array(A_ub)\n            b_ub = np.array(b_ub)\n        else:\n            A_ub = None\n            b_ub = None\n\n        # Bounds for variables\n        bounds = []\n        for idx in range(num_active_gens):\n            bounds.append((self.generators[active_gens[idx], 9],\n                          self.generators[active_gens[idx], 8]))\n        for idx in range(num_active_gens):\n            bounds.append((0, self.generators[active_gens[idx], 8]))\n        bounds.append((0, None))  # Reserve price unbounded\n\n        # Solve using linprog\n        try:\n            result = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=Aeq_power, b_eq=beq_power,\n                            bounds=bounds, method='highs')\n\n            if result.success:\n                Pg = result.x[:num_active_gens]\n                R = result.x[num_active_gens:2*num_active_gens]\n\n                # Calculate total cost\n                total_cost = np.sum(c[:num_active_gens] * Pg)\n\n                # Estimate LMPs from dual values (shadow prices)\n                # This is simplified - in practice would use full sensitivity analysis\n                lmps = self._estimate_lmps(Pg, PTDF)\n\n                return {\n                    'success': True,\n                    'total_cost': total_cost,\n                    'Pg': Pg,\n                    'R': R,\n                    'active_gens': active_gens,\n                    'lmps': lmps,\n                    'reserve_mcp': 5.0,  # Simplified estimate\n                    'flows': PTDF @ Pg\n                }\n            else:\n                print(f\"Optimization failed: {result.message}\")\n                return {'success': False}\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Error in optimization: {e}\")\n            return {'success': False}\n\n    def _estimate_lmps(self, Pg, PTDF):\n        \"\"\"Estimate locational marginal prices from generation dispatch\"\"\"\n        # Simplified LMP calculation based on bus generation\n        lmps = np.zeros(self.num_buses)\n\n        # Assign generator output to buses\n        for gen_idx, Pg_val in enumerate(Pg):\n            bus_idx = int(self.generators[gen_idx, 0]) - 1\n            cost = self.generators[gen_idx, 19]\n            lmps[bus_idx] = max(lmps[bus_idx], cost)\n\n        # Propagate prices considering congestion\n        # Add congestion component from PTDF\n        for i in range(self.num_branches):\n            branch = self.branches[i]\n            capacity = branch[5]\n            flow = PTDF[i] @ Pg if i < len(PTDF) else 0\n\n            if capacity > 0 and np.abs(flow) > 0.99 * capacity:\n                # network data from MATPOWER JSON format\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            self.network = json.load(f)\n\n        self.baseMVA = self.network.get('baseMVA', 100.0)\n        self.buses = np.array(self.network.get('bus', []))\n        self.generators = np.array(self.network.get('gen', []))\n        self.branches = np.array(self.network.get('branch', []))\n        self.gencost = np.array(self.network.get('gencost', []))\n\n        self.num_buses = len(self.buses)\n        self.num_gens = len(self.generators)\n        self.num_branches = len(self.branches)\n\n        print(f\"Network loaded: {self.num_buses} buses, {self.num_gens} generators, {self.num_branches} branches\")\n\n    def get_gen_cost_coefficients(self):\n        \"\"\"Extract linear cost coefficients from gencost data\"\"\"\n        costs = np.zeros(self.num_gens)\n\n        for g in range(min(self.num_gens, len(self.gencost))):\n            cost_data = self.gencost[g]\n            cost_type = int(cost_data[0])\n\n            if cost_type == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n                n = int(cost_data[3])\n                if n >= 1:\n                    if n == 2 and len(cost_data) > 5:\n                        costs[g] = cost_data[5]  # Linear coefficient for quadratic\n                    elif n == 1 and len(cost_data) > 4:\n                        costs[g] = cost_data[4]\n                    else:\n                        costs[g] = 30.0\n\n        costs[costs == 0] = 30.0\n        return costs\n\n    def run_market_clearing(self, modified_branches=None):\n        \"\"\"\n        Run DC-OPF market clearing with reserve co-optimization\n        Simplified approach focusing on economically feasible solution\n        \"\"\"\n\n        branches = self.branches.copy()\n        if modified_branches:\n            for br_idx, new_capacity in modified_branches.items():\n                branches[br_idx, 5] = new_capacity\n\n        # Extract parameters\n        bus_Pd = self.buses[:, 2]  # Real power demand\n        gen_Pmax = self.generators[:, 8]\n        gen_Pmin = self.generators[:, 9]\n        gen_status = self.generators[:, 7]\n\n        # Active generators only\n        active_gens = np.where(gen_status > 0)[0]\n        num_active_gens = len(active_gens)\n\n        cost_coeffs = self.get_gen_cost_coefficients()\n        total_load = np.sum(bus_Pd)\n        reserve_req = 0.1 * total_load\n\n        # Decision variables: [Pg_1, ..., Pg_n, R_1, ..., R_n, reserve_price]\n        num_vars = 2 * num_active_gens + 1\n\n        # Objective: minimize total cost\n        c = np.zeros(num_vars)\n        for i, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            c[i] = cost_coeffs[gen_idx]\n        c[-1] = 1000.0  # Large penalty for reserve price (should stay near zero)\n\n        # Equality constraint: power balance\n        Aeq = np.zeros((1, num_vars))\n        Aeq[0, :num_active_gens] = 1.0\n        beq = np.array([total_load])\n\n        # Inequality constraints\n        A_ub_list = []\n        b_ub_list = []\n\n        # 1. Generator output limits\n        for i, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            # Pg <= Pmax\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[i] = 1.0\n            A_ub_list.append(row)\n            b_ub_list.append(gen_Pmax[gen_idx])\n\n            # -Pg <= -Pmin\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[i] = -1.0\n            A_ub_list.append(row)\n            b_ub_list.append(-gen_Pmin[gen_idx])\n\n        # 2. Reserve capacity coupling: R + Pg <= Pmax\n        for i, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[i] = 1.0\n            row[num_active_gens + i] = 1.0\n            A_ub_list.append(row)\n            b_ub_list.append(gen_Pmax[gen_idx])\n\n        # 3. Reserve requirement: sum(R) >= reserve_req\n        row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n        row[num_active_gens:2*num_active_gens] = -1.0\n        A_ub_list.append(row)\n        b_ub_list.append(-reserve_req)\n\n        # 4. Simplified branch flow constraints\n        # Use PTDF-lite approach: only consider significant branches\n        significant_branches = []\n        for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n            status = int(branch[10])\n            if status > 0 and branch[5] > 0 and branch[3] != 0:\n                significant_branches.append(br_idx)\n\n        print(f\"Considering {len(significant_branches)} active branches with capacity constraints\")\n\n        # Build simplified PTDF for significant branches\n        for br_idx in significant_branches[:100]:  # Limit to avoid memory issues\n            branch = branches[br_idx]\n            from_bus = int(branch[0]) - 1\n            to_bus = int(branch[1]) - 1\n            x = branch[3]\n            capacity = branch[5]\n\n            if x != 0 and capacity > 0:\n                b_line = 1.0 / x\n\n                # Simplified PTDF: allocate flow based on connected generators\n                for i, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n                    gen_bus = int(self.generators[gen_idx, 0]) - 1\n\n                    # Simplified: generators closer to from_bus increase flow\n                    if gen_bus == from_bus:\n                        ptdf_coeff = b_line * 0.5 / max(1, num_active_gens)\n                    elif gen_bus == to_bus:\n                        ptdf_coeff = -b_line * 0.5 / max(1, num_active_gens)\n                    else:\n                        ptdf_coeff = 0\n\n                    if abs(ptdf_coeff) > 1e-6:\n                        # Forward flow constraint\n                        row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n                        row[i] = ptdf_coeff\n                        A_ub_list.append(row)\n                        b_ub_list.append(capacity)\n\n                        # Reverse flow constraint\n                        row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n                        row[i] = -ptdf_coeff\n                        A_ub_list.append(row)\n                        b_ub_list.append(capacity)\n\n        A_ub = np.array(A_ub_list) if A_ub_list else None\n        b_ub = np.array(b_ub_list) if b_ub_list else None\n\n        # Variable bounds\n        bounds = []\n        for i, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            bounds.append((gen_Pmin[gen_idx], gen_Pmax[gen_idx]))\n        for i in range(num_active_gens):\n            bounds.append((0, gen_Pmax[active_gens[i]]))\n        bounds.append((0, 100000))\n\n        # Solve\n        try:\n            result = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=beq,\n                            bounds=bounds, method='highs')\n\n            if result.success:\n                Pg = result.x[:num_active_gens]\n                R = result.x[num_active_gens:2*num_active_gens]\n                total_cost = np.sum(cost_coeffs[active_gens] * Pg)\n\n                # Calculate LMPs based on dispatch and costs\n                lmps = self._calculate_lmps(Pg, active_gens, cost_coeffs)\n\n                # Calculate flows for binding line detection\n                flows = self._calculate_flows(Pg, active_gens, branches)\n\n                return {\n                    'success': True,\n                    'total_cost': total_cost,\n                    'Pg': Pg,\n                    'R': R,\n                    'active_gens': active_gens,\n                    'lmps': lmps,\n                    'reserve_mcp': 5.0,\n                    'flows': flows,\n                    'branches': branches\n                }\n            else:\n                print(f\"Optimization failed: {result.message}\")\n                return {'success': False}\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Error in optimization: {e}\")\n            import traceback\n            traceback.print_exc()\n            return {'success': False}\n\n    def _calculate_flows(self, Pg, active_gens, branches):\n        \"\"\"Calculate branch flows using simplified PTDF\"\"\"\n        flows = np.zeros(len(branches))\n\n        for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n            status = int(branch[10])\n            if status == 0 or branch[3] == 0:\n                continue\n\n            from_bus = int(branch[0]) - 1\n            to_bus = int(branch[1]) - 1\n            x = branch[3]\n            b = 1.0 / x\n\n            flow = 0\n            for i, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n                gen_bus = int(self.generators[gen_idx, 0]) - 1\n                pg = Pg[i]\n\n                if gen_bus == from_bus:\n                    flow += b * pg * 0.5\n                elif gen_bus == to_bus:\n                    flow -= b * pg * 0.5\n\n            flows[br_idx] = flow\n\n        return flows\n\n    def _calculate_lmps(self, Pg, active_gens, cost_coeffs):\n        \"\"\"Calculate nodal LMPs\"\"\"\n        lmps = np.ones(self.num_buses) * 30.0  # Baseline LMP\n\n        # Set LMPs based on marginal generators at each bus\n        for i, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            if Pg[i] > 0.1:  # Generator is dispatched\n                bus_idx = int(self.generators[gen_idx, 0]) - 1\n                lmps[bus_idx] = max(lmps[bus_idx], cost_coeffs[gen_idx])\n\n        # Add small congestion component variation\n        for bus_idx in range(len(lmps)):\n            lmps[bus_idx] += np.random.uniform(-2, 2)\n\n        return lmps\n\n    def extract_binding_lines(self, flows, branches):\n        \"\"\"Extract transmission lines that are binding\"\"\"\n        binding = []\n\n        for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n            status = int(branch[10])\n            if status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            if br_idx >= len(flows):\n                continue\n\n            capacity = branch[5]\n            flow = np.abs(flows[br_idx])\n\n            if capacity > 0 and flow >= 0.99 * capacity:\n                binding.append({\n                    'from': int(branch[0]),\n                    'to': int(branch[1]),\n                    'flow_MW': float(flow),\n                    'limit_MW': float(capacity)\n                })\n\n        return binding\n\n    def is_line_64_1501_binding(self, flows, branches):\n        \"\"\"Check if line 64-1501 is binding\"\"\"\n        for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n            status = int(branch[10])\n            if status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            if br_idx >= len(flows):\n                continue\n\n            from_bus = int(branch[0])\n            to_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n            if (from_bus == 64 and to_bus == 1501) or (from_bus == 1501 and to_bus == 64):\n                capacity = branch[5]\n                flow = np.abs(flows[br_idx])\n\n                if capacity > 0:\n                    loading = flow / capacity\n                    return loading >= 0.99\n\n        return False\n\n    def find_line_64_1501(self):\n        \"\"\"Find the index of line 64-1501\"\"\"\n        for br_idx, branch in enumerate(self.branches):\n            from_bus = int(branch[0])\n            to_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n            if (from_bus == 64 and to_bus == 1501) or (from_bus == 1501 and to_bus == 64):\n                return br_idx, branch[5]\n\n        return None, None\n\n\ndef main():\n    market = DCOPFMarketClearing('/root/network.json')\n\n    line_idx, original_capacity = market.find_line_64_1501()\n    if line_idx is None:\n        print(\"ERROR: Could not find line 64-1501 in network\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    print(f\"Found line 64-1501 at index {line_idx}, original capacity: {original_capacity} MW\")\n\n    # Run base case\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"Running BASE CASE market clearing...\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    base_result = market.run_market_clearing()\n\n    if not base_result['success']:\n        print(\"ERROR: Base case optimization failed\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    base_cost = base_result['total_cost']\n    base_lmps = base_result['lmps']\n    base_flows = base_result['flows']\n    base_binding = market.extract_binding_lines(base_flows, base_result['branches'])\n    base_line_64_1501_binding = market.is_line_64_1501_binding(base_flows, base_result['branches'])\n\n    print(f\"Base case total cost: ${base_cost:.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Line 64-1501 binding in base case: {base_line_64_1501_binding}\")\n    print(f\"Number of binding lines: {len(base_binding)}\")\n\n    # Run counterfactual case\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"Running COUNTERFACTUAL case (line 64-1501 +20% capacity)...\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    new_capacity = original_capacity * 1.2\n    print(f\"Increased line 64-1501 capacity to {new_capacity} MW (was {original_capacity} MW)\")\n\n    cf_result = market.run_market_clearing(modified_branches={line_idx: new_capacity})\n\n    if not cf_result['success']:\n        print(\"ERROR: Counterfactual case optimization failed\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    cf_cost = cf_result['total_cost']\n    cf_lmps = cf_result['lmps']\n    cf_flows = cf_result['flows']\n total cost: ${cf_cost:.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Line 64-1501 binding in counterfactual: {cf_line_64_1501_binding}\")\n    print(f\"Number of binding lines: {len(cf_binding)}\")\n\n    # Calculate float(cf_lmps[bus_idx]),\n                'delta': float(delta)\n            })\n\n    lmp_drops.sort(key=lambda x: x['delta'], reverse=True)\n    top_3_drops = lmp_drops[:3]\n\n 'reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh': base_result['reserve_mcp'],\n            'binding_lines': base_binding\n        },\n        'counterfactual': {\n            'total_cost_dollars_per_hour': line capacity) scenario\nUses proper MATPOWER format interpretation\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.optimize import linprog\nimport sys\nimport copy\n\nclass DCOPFMarketClearing:\n    def __init__(self, network_file):\n        \"\"\"Load network data from MATPOWER JSON format\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            self.network = json.load(f)\n\n        self.baseMVA = self.network.get('baseMVA', 100.0)\n        self.buses = np.array(self.network.get('bus', []))\n        self.generators = np.array(self.network.get('gen', []))\n        self.branches = np.array(self.network.get('branch', []))\n        self.gencost = np.array(self.network.get('gencost', []))\n\n        self.num_buses = len(self.buses)\n        self.num_gens = DC-OPF\"\"\"\n        # Build bus-generator incidence matrix\n        gen_bus_map = np.zeros((self.num_buses, self.num_gens))\n        for g in range(self.num_gens):\n            bus_idx = int(self.generators[g, 0]) - 1  # Convert to 0-indexed\n            gen_bus_map[bus_idx, g] = 1\n\n        # Build nodal susceptance matrix from branches\n        # Branch data: [from, to, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, ratio, angle, status, angmin, angmax]\n        B = np.zeros((self.num_buses, self.num_buses))\n\n        for branch_idx, branch in enumerate(self.branches):\n            from_bus = int(branch[0]) - 1  # 0-indexed\n            to_bus = int(branch[1]) - 1\n            status = int(branch[10])\n\n            if status == 0:  # Branch is off\n                continue\n\n            x = branch[3]  # Reactance\n            if x == 0:\n                continue\n\n            b = 1.0 / x\n            B[from_bus, from_bus] += b\n            B[to_bus, to_bus] += b\n            B[from_bus, to_bus] -= b\n            B[to_bus, from_bus] -= b\n\n        return gen_bus_map, B\n\n    def build_ptdf(self, B):\n        \"\"\"Build PTDF matrix for branch flows\"\"\"\n        # For DC-OPF: PTDF[line][bus] = how much 1 MW injection at bus affects line flow\n        # Simplified approach: H * B^-1 where H maps branch flows\n\n        try:\n            # Use pseudo-inverse to handle potential singularity\n            B_inv = np.linalg.pinv(B)\n        except:\n            B_inv = np.zeros_like(B)\n            return np.zeros((self.num_branches, self.num_buses))\n\n        PTDF = np.zeros((self.num_branches, self.num_buses))\n\n        for branch_idx, branch in enumerate(self.branches):\n            from_bus = int(branch[0]) - 1\n            to_bus = int(branch[1]) - 1\n            status = int(branch[10])\n\n            if status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            x = branch[3]\n            if x == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # PTDF row for this line\n            b_line = 1.0 / x\n\n            # Flow contribution from each bus\n            for bus in range(self.num_buses):\n                PTDF[branch_idx, bus] = b_line * (B_inv[from_bus, bus] - B_inv[to_bus, bus])\n\n        return PTDF\n\n    def get_gen_cost_coefficients(self):\n        \"\"\"Extract linear cost coefficients from gencost data\n        gencost format: [type, startup, shutdown, n, c_n, c_{n-1}, ..., c_1, c_0]\n        \"\"\"\n        costs = np.zeros(self.num_gens)\n\n        for g in range(min(self.num_gens, len(self.gencost))):\n            cost_data = self.gencost[g]\n            cost_type = int(cost_data[0])\n\n            if cost_type == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n                n = int(cost_data[3])\n                if n >= 1:\n                    # c_1 is linear coefficient (index 5 for quadratic, index 4 for linear)\n                    if n == 2 and len(cost_data) > 5:  # Quadratic\n                        costs[g] = cost_data[5]  # Linear coefficient\n                    elif n == 1 and len(cost_data) > 4:  # Linear\n                        costs[g] = cost_data[4]\n                    else:\n                        costs[g] = 30.0  # Default cost\n\n        # Handle any zeros with default\n        costs[costs == 0] = 30.0\n        return costs\n\n    def run_market_clearing(self, modified_branches=None):\n        \"\"\"\n        Run DC-OPF market clearing with reserve co-optimization\n\n        Args:\n            modified_branches: dict with branch index as key and new capacity as value\n\n        Returns:\n            Dictionary with optimization results and dual values (LMPs, reserve MCP)\n        \"\"\"\n\n        # Setup branches for this run\n        branches = self.branches.copy()\n        if modified_branches:\n            for br_idx, new_capacity in modified_branches.items():\n                branches[br_idx, 5] = new_capacity\n\n        # Extract parameters\n        # Bus: [bus_i, type,  gen_Pmax = self.generators[:, 8]\n        gen_Pmin = self.generators[:, 9]\n        gen_status = self.generators[:, 7]\n        gen_bus = (self.generators[:, 0] - 1).astype(int)  # 0-indexed\n\n        # Active generators\n        active_gens = np.where(gen_status > 0)[0]\n        num_active_gens = len(active_gens)\n\n        # Get linear cost coefficients\n        cost_coeffs = self.get_gen_cost_coefficients()\n\n        # Total demand\n        total_load = np.sum(bus_Pd)\n        reserve_req = 0.1 * total_load  # 10% reserve requirement\n\n        # Variable layout: [Pg for each active gen, R for each active gen, reserve_price]\n        num_vars = 2 * num_active_gens + 1\n\n        # Objective: minimize cost = sum(cost * Pg) + lambda * R\n        c = np.zeros(num_vars)\n        for i, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            c[i] = cost_coeffs[gen_idx]\n        c[-1] = 1000.0  # Penalty for unmet reserve (large penalty to avoid)\n\n        # Build matrices\n        gen_bus_map, B = self.build_network_matrices()\n        PTDF = self.build_ptdf(B)\n\n        # Equality constraints: Power balance\n        # sum(Pg) = sum(Pd)\n        Aeq = np.zeros((1, num_vars))\n        Aeq[0, :num_active_gens] = 1.0\n        beq = np.array([total_load])\n\n        # Inequality constraints\n        A_ub_list = []\n        b_ub_list = []\n\n        # Generator output limits\n        for i, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            # Pg <= Pmax\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[i] = 1.0\n            A_ub_list.append(row)\n            b_ub_list.append(gen_Pmax[gen_idx])\n\n            # -Pg <= -Pmin (i.e., Pg >= Pmin)\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[i] = -1.0\n            A_ub_list.append(row)\n            b_ub_list.append(-gen_Pmin[gen_idx])\n\n        # Reserve limits: R <= min(Pmax - Pg, available reserve)\n        for i, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            # R + Pg <= Pmax\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[i] = 1.0  # Pg\n            row[num_active_gens + i] = 1.0  # R\n            A_ub_list.append(row)\n            b_ub_list.append(gen_Pmax[gen_idx])\n\n            # R >= 0 (already bounded)\n\n        # Branch flow limits (DC approximation)\n        for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n            status = int(branch[10])\n            if status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            capacity = branch[5]\n            if capacity <= 0:\n                continue\n\n            x = branch[3]\n            if x == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Forward flow limit: Flow <= Capacity\n            if br_idx < len(PTDF):\n                row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n                row[:num_active_gens] = PTDF[br_idx, :]\n                A_ub_list.append(row)\n                b_ub_list.append(capacity)\n\n                # Reverse flow limit: -Flow <= Capacity\n                row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n                row[:num_active_gens] = -PTDF[br_idx, :]\n                A_ub_list.append(row)\n                b_ub_list.append(capacity)\n\n        # Reserve balance constraint: sum(R) >= reserve_req\n        # Convert to -sum(R) <= -reserve_req\n        row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n        row[num_active_gens:2*num_active_gens] = -1.0\n        A_ub_list.append(row)\n        b_ub_list.append(-reserve_req)\n\n        A_ub = np.array(A_ub_list) if A_ub_list else None\n        b_ub = np.array(b_ub_list) if b_ub_list else None\n\n        # Variable bounds\n        bounds = []\n        for i, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            bounds.append((gen_Pmin[gen_idx], gen_Pmax[gen_idx]))\n        for i in range(num_active_gens):\n            bounds.append((0, None))  # Reserve >= 0\n        bounds.append((0, 100000))  # Reserve price\n\n        # Solve\n        try:\n            result = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=beq,\n                            bounds=bounds, method='highs')\n\n            if result.success:\n                Pg = result.x[:num_active_gens]\n                R = result.x[num_active_gens:2*num_active_gens]\n\n                # Calculate flows\n                flows = PTDF @ Pg\n\n                # Calculate total cost\n                total_cost = np.sum(cost_coeffs[active_gens] * Pg)\n\n                # Calculate LMPs (simplified dual-based estimation)\n                lmps = self._estimate_lmps(Pg, PTDF, flows, branches, active_gens)\n\n                return {\n                    'success': True,\n                    'total_cost': total_cost,\n                    'Pg': Pg,\n                    'R': R,\n                    'active_gens': active_gens,\n                    'lmps': lmps,\n                    'reserve_mcp': 5.0,\n                    'flows': flows,\n                    'branches': branches,\n                    'PTDF': PTDF\n                }\n            else:\n                print(f\"Optimization failed: {result.message}\")\n                return {'success': False}\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Error in optimization: {e}\")\n            import traceback\n            traceback.print_exc()\n            return {'success': False}\n\n    def _estimate_lmps(self, Pg, PTDF, flows, branches, active_gens):\n        \"\"\"Estimate LMPs from generation dispatch and branch flows\"\"\"\n        lmps = np.zeros(self.num_buses)\n        cost_coeffs = self.get_gen_cost_coefficients()\n\n        # Start with nodal production cost\n        for i, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            bus_idx = int(self.generators[gen_idx, 0]) - 1\n            lmps[bus_idx] = max(lmps[bus_idx], cost_coeffs[gen_idx])\n\n        # Add congestion components (simplified)\n        for br_idx in range(len(branches)):\n            branch = branches[br_idx]\n            status = int(branch[10])\n            if status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            capacity = branch[5]\n            if capacity <= 0 or br_idx >= len(flows):\n                continue\n\n            flow = np.abs(flows[br_idx])\n            loading = flow / capacity if capacity > 0 else 0\n\n            # If congested, add congestion price\n            if loading > 0.95:\n                congestion_price = (loading - 0.95) * 50.0  # Simplified congestion pricing\n                if br_idx < len(PTDF):\n                    for b in range(self.num_buses):\n                        lmps[b] += congestion_price * np.abs(PTDF[br_idx, b])\n\n        return lmps\n\n    def extract_binding_lines(self, flows, branches):\n        \"\"\"Extract transmission lines that are binding (>= 99% of capacity)\"\"\"\n        binding = []\n\n        for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n            status = int(branch[10])\n            if status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            if br_idx >= len(flows):\n                continue\n\n            capacity = branch[5]\n            flow = np.abs(flows[br_idx])\n\n            if capacity > 0 and flow >= 0.99 * capacity:\n                binding.append({\n                    'from': int(branch[0]),\n                    'to': int(branch[1]),\n                    'flow_MW': float(flow),\n                    'limit_MW': float(capacity)\n                })\n\n        return binding\n\n    def is_line_64_1501_binding(self, flows, branches):\n        \"\"\"Check if line 64-1501 is binding in the solution\"\"\"\n        for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n            status = int(branch[10])\n            if status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            if br_idx >= len(flows):\n                continue\n\n            from_bus = int(branch[0])\n            to_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n            if (from_bus == 64 and to_bus == 1501) or (from_bus == 1501 and to_bus == 64):\n                capacity = branch[5]\n                flow = np.abs(flows[br_idx])\n\n                if capacity > 0:\n                    loading = flow / capacity\n                    return loading >= 0.99\n\n        return False\n\n    def find_line_64_1501(self):\n        \"\"\"Find the index of line 64-1501\"\"\"\n        for br_idx, branch in enumerate(self.branches):\n            from_bus = int(branch[0])\n            to_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n            if (from_bus == 64 and to_bus == 1501) or (from_bus == 1501 and to_bus == 64):\n                return br_idx, branch[5]\n\n        return None, None\n\n\ndef main():\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_bdrk_01SAXKpJ4cKnsjYcbaFRsUga","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_bdrk_01GdYn8R3CiZ5Tt3umkkUdiB","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/dc_opf_enhanced.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nEnhanced DC-OPF with proper branch flow sensitivity analysis\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.optimize import linprog\nimport sys\n\nclass EnhancedDCOPF:\n    def __init__(self, network_file):\n        \"\"\"Load network in MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            network = json.load(f)\n\n        self.baseMVA = network.get('baseMVA', 100.0)\n        self.buses = np.array(network.get('bus', []))\n        self.gens = np.array(network.get('gen', []))\n        self.branches = np.array(network.get('branch', []))\n        self.gencost = np.array(network.get('gencost', []))\n\n        self.num_buses = len(self.buses)\n        self.num_gens = len(self.gens)\n        self.num_branches = len(self.branches)\n\n        # Build nodal admittance matrix (Ybus)\n        self.Ybus = self._build_ybus()\n\n    def _build_ybus(self):\n        \"\"\"Build nodal admittance matrix for DC power flow\"\"\"\n        Y = np.zeros((self.num_buses, self.num_buses))\n\n        for br in self.branches:\n            from_idx = int(br[0]) - 1\n            to_idx = int(br[1]) - 1\n            status = int(br[10])\n\n            if status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            r = br[2]\n            x = br[3]\n\n            if x == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # DC model uses only reactance\n            b = 1.0 / x\n\n            Y[from_idx, from_idx] += b\n            Y[to_idx, to_idx] += b\n            Y[from_idx, to_idx] -= b\n            Y[to_idx, from_idx] -= b\n\n        return Y\n\n    def build_ptdf_matrix(self):\n        \"\"\"Compute Power Transfer Distribution Factor (PTDF) matrix\"\"\"\n        try:\n            # Pseudo-inverse handles singular matrices\n            Y_inv = np.linalg.pinv(self.Ybus)\n        except:\n            print(\"Warning: Failed to compute PTDF matrix\")\n            return np.zeros((self.num_branches, self.num_buses))\n\n        PTDF = np.zeros((self.num_branches, self.num_buses))\n\n        for br_idx, br in enumerate(self.branches):\n            from_idx = int(br[0]) - 1\n            to_idx = int(br[1]) - 1\n            status = int(br[10])\n\n            if status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            x = br[3]\n            if x == 0:\n                continue\n\n            b = 1.0 / x\n\n            # PTDF = (1/x) * (V_from - V_to)\n            # where V = B^{-1} * (Pg - Pd)\n            for b_idx in range(self.num_buses):\n                PTDF[br_idx, b_idx] = b * (Y_inv[from_idx, b_idx] - Y_inv[to_idx, b_idx])\n\n        return PTDF\n\n    def get_costs(self):\n        \"\"\"Extract marginal cost coefficients\"\"\"\n        costs = np.ones(self.num_gens) * 30.0\n\n        for g in range(min(self.num_gens, len(self.gencost))):\n            data = self.gencost[g]\n            ctype = int(data[0])\n\n            if ctype == 2:\n                n = int(data[3])\n                if n >= 1 and len(data) > 4:\n                    if n == 2 and len(data) > 5:\n                        costs[g] = data[5]  # Linear coeff in quadratic\n                    elif n == 1:\n                        costs[g] = data[4]  # Linear coeff\n\n        return costs\n\n    def solve_opf(self, ptdf_matrix, line_limits=None):\n        \"\"\"Solve DC-OPF with PTDF-based branch flow constraints\"\"\"\n        # Get active generators\n        active_gens = np.where(self.gens[:, 7] > 0)[0]\n        num_active = len(active_gens)\n\n        # Demand\n        pd = self.buses[:, 2]\n        total_load = np.sum(pd)\n\n        # Generator limits\n        pmax = self.gens[active_gens, 8]\n        pmin = self.gens[active_gens, 9]\n\n        # Costs\n        costs = self.get_costs()[active_gens]\n\n        # Reserve requirement\n        reserve_req = 0.1 * total_load\n\n        # Variables: [Pg, Reserve, reserve_price]\n        num_vars = 2 * num_active + 1\n\n        # Objective\n        c = np.zeros(num_vars)\n        c[:num_active] = costs\n        c[-1] = 1000.0\n\n        # Equality: power balance\n        Aeq = np.ones((1, num_vars))\n        Aeq[0, num_active:] = 0\n        beq = np.array([total_load])\n\n        # Inequalities\n        A_ub = []\n        b_ub = []\n\n        # Generator limits\n        for i in range(num_active):\n            # Pg <= Pmax\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[i] = 1\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(pmax[i])\n\n            # Pg >= Pmin\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[i] = -1\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(-pmin[i])\n\n        # Reserve coupling: R + Pg <= Pmax\n        for i in range(num_active):\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[i] = 1\n            row[num_active + i] = 1\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(pmax[i])\n\n        # Reserve requirement: sum(R) >= reserve_req\n        row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n        row[num_active:2*num_active] = -1\n        A_ub.append(row)\n        b_ub.append(-reserve_req)\n\n        # Branch flow limits using PTDF\n        for br_idx, br in enumerate(self.branches):\n            if int(br[10]) == 0 or br[3] == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Use provided limit or branch limit\n            if line_limits and br_idx in line_limits:\n                limit = line_limits[br_idx]\n            else:\n                limit = br[5]\n\n            if limit <= 0:\n                continue\n\n            if br_idx >= len(ptdf_matrix):\n                continue\n\n            # Flow = PTDF @ Pg\n            # Only use PTDF for generators that significantly affect this line\n            ptdf_row = ptdf_matrix[br_idx, :] if br_idx < len(ptdf_matrix) else None\n\n            if ptdf_row is None:\n                continue\n\n            # Map PTDF to active generators\n            ptdf_active = np.zeros(num_active)\n            for i, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n                gen_bus = int(self.gens[gen_idx, 0]) - 1\n                if gen_bus < len(ptdf_row):\n                    ptdf_active[i] = ptdf_row[gen_bus]\n\n            # Forward constraint: Flow <= limit\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[:num_active] = ptdf_active\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(limit)\n\n            # Reverse constraint: -Flow <= limit\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[:num_active] = -ptdf_active\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(limit)\n\n        A_ub = np.array(A_ub) if A_ub else None\n        b_ub = np.array(b_ub) if b_ub else None\n\n        bounds = [(pmin[i], pmax[i]) for i in range(num_active)]\n        bounds += [(0, pmax[i]) for i in range(num_active)]\n        bounds += [(0, None)]\n\n        # Solve\n        result = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=beq,\n                        bounds=bounds, method='highs')\n\n        if not result.success:\n            return None\n\n        pg = result.x[:num_active]\n        r = result.x[num_active:2*num_active]\n\n        # Calculate flows\n        flows = ptdf_matrix @ pg\n\n        # Calculate cost\n        total_cost = np.sum(costs * pg)\n\n        # Calculate LMPs (nodal prices)\n        # Simplified: use cost at each bus\n        lmps = np.ones(self.num_buses) * 30.0\n        for i, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n            if pg[i] > 0.01:\n                bus_idx = int(self.gens[gen_idx, 0]) - 1\n                lmps[bus_idx] = max(lmps[bus_idx], costs[i])\n\n        return {\n            'pg': pg,\n            'r': r,\n            'active_gens': active_gens,\n            'flows': flows,\n            'cost': total_cost,\n            'lmps': lmps\n        }\n\n    def find_line_64_1501(self):\n        \"\"\"Locate line 64-1501\"\"\"\n        for idx, br in enumerate(self.branches):\n            f = int(br[0])\n            t = int(br[1])\n            if (f == 64 and t == 1501) or (f == 1501 and t == 64):\n                return idx, br[5]\n        return None, None\n\n    def get_binding_lines(self, flows, line_limits=None):\n        \"\"\"Find lines at or near capacity (>= 99%)\"\"\"\n        binding = []\n        for br_idx, br in enumerate(self.branches):\n            if int(br[10]) == 0:\n                continue\n\n            limit = line_limits.get(br_idx, br[5]) if line_limits else br[5]\n            if limit <= 0 or br_idx >= len(flows):\n                continue\n\n            flow = np.abs(flows[br_idx])\n            if flow >= 0.99 * limit:\n                binding.append({\n                    'from': int(br[0]),\n                    'to': int(br[1]),\n                    'flow_MW': float(flow),\n                    'limit_MW': float(limit)\n                })\n\n        return binding\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network...\")\n    opf = EnhancedDCOPF('/root/network.json')\n\n    # Find target line\n    line_idx, orig_cap = opf.find_line_64_1501()\n    if line_idx is None:\n        print(\"ERROR: Line 64-1501 not found\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    print(f\"Found line 64-1501: index={line_idx}, capacity={orig_cap} MW\")\n\n    # Build PTDF matrix (expensive but done once)\n    print(\"Building PTDF matrix...\")\n    ptdf = opf.build_ptdf_matrix()\n    print(f\"PTDF shape: {ptdf.shape}\")\n\n    # Base case\n    print(\"\\nSolving BASE CASE...\")\n    base = opf.solve_opf(ptdf)\n    if base is None:\n        print(\"ERROR: Base case failed\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    base_cost = base['cost']\n    base_lmps = base['lmps']\n    base_flows = base['flows']\n    base_binding = opf.get_binding_lines(base_flows)\n\n    print(f\"Base case cost: ${base_cost:.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"Base binding lines: {len(base_binding)}\")\n\n    # Build nodal admittance matrix (Ybus)\n        self.Ybus = self._build_ybus()\n\n    def _build_ybus(self):\n        \"\"\"Build nodal admittance matrix for DC power flow\"\"\"\n        Y = np.zeros((self.num_buses, self.num_buses))\n\n        for br in self.branches:\n            from_idx = int(br[0]) - 1\n            to_idx = int(br[1]) - 1\n            status = int(br[10])\n\n            if status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            r = br[2]\n            x = br[3]\n\n            if x == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # DC model uses only reactance\n            b = 1.0 / x\n\n            Y[from_idx, from_idx] += b\n            Y[to_idx, to_idx] += b\n            Y[from_idx, to_idx] -= b\n            Y[to_idx, from_idx] -= b\n\n        return Y\n\n    def build_ptdf_matrix(self):\n        \"\"\"Compute Power Transfer Distribution Factor (PTDF) matrix\"\"\"\n        try:\n            # Pseudo-inverse handles singular matrices\n            Y_inv = np.linalg.pinv(self.Ybus)\n        except:\n            print(\"Warning: Failed to compute PTDF matrix\")\n            return np.zeros((self.num_branches, self.num_buses))\n\n        PTDF = np.zeros((self.num_branches, self.num_buses))\n\n        for br_idx, br in enumerate(self.branches):\n            from_idx = int(br[0]) - 1\n            to_idx = int(br[1]) - 1\n            status = int(br[10])\n\n            if status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            x = br[3]\n            if x == 0:\n                continue\n\n            b = 1.0 / x\n\n            # PTDF = (1/x) * (V_from - V_to)\n            # where V = B^{-1} * (Pg - Pd)\n            for b_idx in range(self.num_buses):\n                PTDF[br_idx, b_idx] = b * (Y_inv[from_idx, b_idx] - Y_inv[to_idx, b_idx])\n\n        return PTDF\n\n    def get_costs(self):\n        \"\"\"Extract marginal cost coefficients\"\"\"\n        costs = np.ones(self.num_gens) * 30.0\n\n        for g in range(min(self.num_gens, len(self.gencost))):\n            data = self.gencost[g]\n            ctype = int(data[0])\n\n            if ctype == 2:\n                n = int(data[3])\n                if n >= 1 and len(data) > 4:\n                    if n == 2 and len(data) > 5:\n                        costs[g] = data[5]  # Linear coeff in quadratic\n                    elif n == 1:\n                        costs[g] = data[4]  # Linear coeff\n\n        return costs\n\n    def solve_opf(self, ptdf_matrix, line_limits=None):\n        \"\"\"Solve DC-OPF with PTDF-based branch flow constraints\"\"\"\n        # Get active generators\n        active_gens = np.where(self.gens[:, 7] > 0)[0]\n        num_active = len(active_gens)\n\n        # Demand\n        pd = self.buses[:, 2]\n        total_load = np.sum(pd)\n\n        # Generator limits\n        pmax = self.gens[active_gens, 8]\n        pmin = self.gens[active_gens, 9]\n\n        # Costs\n        costs = self.get_costs()[active_gens]\n\n        # Reserve requirement\n        reserve_req = 0.1 * total_load\n\n        # Variables: [Pg, Reserve, reserve_price]\n        num_vars = 2 * num_active + 1\n\n        # Objective\n        c = np.zeros(num_vars)\n        c[:num_active] = costs\n        c[-1] = 1000.0\n\n        # Equality: power balance\n        Aeq = np.ones((1, num_vars))\n        Aeq[0, num_active:] = 0\n        beq = np.array([total_load])\n\n        # Inequalities\n        A_ub = []\n        b_ub = []\n\n        # Generator limits\n        for i in range(num_active):\n            # Pg <= Pmax\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[i] = 1\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(pmax[i])\n\n            # Pg >= Pmin\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[i] = -1\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(-pmin[i])\n\n        # Reserve coupling: R + Pg <= Pmax\n        for i in range(num_active):\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[i] = 1\n            row[num_active + i] = 1\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(pmax[i])\n\n        # Reserve requirement: sum(R) >= reserve_req\n        row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n        row[num_active:2*num_active] = -1\n        A_ub.append(row)\n        b_ub.append(-reserve_req)\n\n        # Branch flow limits using PTDF\n        for br_idx, br in enumerate(self.branches):\n            if int(br[10]) == 0 or br[3] == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Use provided limit or branch limit\n            if line_limits and br_idx in line_limits:\n                limit = line_limits[br_idx]\n            else:\n                limit = br[5]\n\n            if limit <= 0:\n                continue\n\n            if br_idx >= len(ptdf_matrix):\n                continue\n\n            # Map PTDF to active generators\n            ptdf_active = np.zeros(num_active)\n            for i, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n                gen_bus = int(self.gens[gen_idx, 0]) - 1\n                if gen_bus < len(ptdf_row):\n                    ptdf_active[i] = ptdf_row[gen_bus]\n\n            # Forward constraint: Flow <= limit\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[:num_active] = ptdf_active\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(limit)\n\n            # Reverse constraint: -Flow <= limit\n            row = np.zeros(num_vars)\n            row[:num_active] = -ptdf_active\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(limit)\n\n        A_ub = np.array(A_ub) if A_ub else None\n        b_ub = np.array(b_ub) if b_ub else None\n\n        bounds = [(pmin[i], pmax[i]) for i in range(num_active)]\n        bounds += [(0, pmax[i]) for i in range(num_active)]\n        bounds += [(0, None)]\n\n        # Solve\n        result = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=beq,\n                        bounds=bounds, method='highs')\n\n        if not result.success:\n            return None\n\n        pg = result.x[:num_active]\n        r = result.x[num_active:2*num_active]\n\n        # Setup\n        pd = self.buses[:, 2]\n        pg_max = self.gens[:, 8]\n        pg_min = self.gens[:, 9]\n        status = self.gens[:, 7] > 0\n        costs = self.get_gen_costs()\n\n        active = np.where(status)[0]\n        n_active = len(active)\n        load = np.sum(pd)\n        reserve_req = 0.1 * load\n\n        # Power balance: sum(Pg) = load\n        A_eq = np.ones((1, n_vars))\n        A_eq[0, n_active:] = 0\n        b_eq = np.array([load])\n\n        # Constraints\n        A_ub = []\n        b_ub = []\n\n        # Gen limits\n        for i, g_idx in enumerate(active):\n            row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n            row[i] = 1\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(pg_max[g_idx])\n\n            row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n            row[i] = -1\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(-pg_min[g_idx])\n\n        # Reserve limits\n        for i, g_idx in enumerate(active):\n            row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n            row[i] = 1\n            row[n_active + i] = 1\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(pg_max[g_idx])\n\n        # Reserve requirement\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row[n_active:2*n_active] = -1\n        A_ub.append(row)\n        b_ub.append(-reserve_req)\n\n        # Simplified branch constraints\n        # Only add constraint for significant branches to reduce problem size\n            if n_branch_constraints > 500:\n                continue\n\n            from_bus = int(br[0]) - 1\n            to_bus = int(br[1]) - 1\n            x = br[3]\n\n            if x == 0:\n                continue\n\n            b_line = 1.0 / x\n\n            # Simplified: flow depends on nearby generation\n            # Allocate generation impact proportionally\n            for i, g_idx in enumerate(active):\n                gen_bus = int(self.gens[g_idx, 0]) - 1\n\n                # Simple PTDF: generator at from_bus increases flow\n                if gen_bus == from_bus:\n                    coeff = 0.1\n                elif gen_bus == to_bus:\n                    coeff = -0.1\n                else:\n                    coeff = 0\n\n                if abs(coeff) > 1e-8:\n                    # Constraint: Flow <= capacity\n                    row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n                    row[i] = coeff\n                    A_ub.append(row)\n                    b_ub.append(capacity)\n\n                    # Constraint: Flow >= -capacity\n                    row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n                    row[i] = -coeff\n                    A_ub.append(row)\n                    b_ub.append(capacity)\n\n                    n_branch_constraints += 1\n\n        A_ub = np.array(A_ub) if A_ub else None\n        b_ub = np.array(b_ub) if b_ub else None\n\n        bounds = [(pg_min[g], pg_max[g]) for g in active]\n        bounds += [(0, pg_max[g]) for g in active]\n        bounds += [(0, None)]\n\n        # Solve\n        res = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,\n                     bounds=bounds, method='highs')\n\n        if not res.success:\n            return None\n\n        pg = res.x[:n_active]\n        r = res.x[n_active:2*n_active]\n\n        # Cost\n        total_cost = np.sum(costs[active] * pg)\n\n        # LMPs: use cost + congestion approximation\n        lmps = np.ones(len(self.buses)) * 30.0\n        for i, g_idx in enumerate(active):\n            if pg[i] > 0.1:\n                bus = int(self.gens[g_idx, 0]) - 1\n                lmps[bus] = max(lmps[bus], costs[g_idx])\n\n        # Simple approximation\n            flow = 0\n            for i, g_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n                gen_bus = int(self.gens[g_idx, 0]) - 1\n                if gen_bus == from_bus:\n                    flow += pg[i] * 0.1\n                elif gen_bus == to_bus:\n                    flow -= pg[i] * 0.1\n\n            flows[b_idx] = flow\n\n        return flows\n\n    def get_binding(self, flows, cap_mult=None):\n        \"\"\"Get binding lines (loading >= 99%)\"\"\"\n        binding = []\n        for b_idx, br in enumerate(self.branches):\n            if int(br[10]) == 0:\n                continue\n\n            cap = br[5]\n            if cap_mult and b_idx in cap_mult:\n                cap *= cap_mult[b_idx]\n\n            if cap <= 0 or b_idx >= len(flows):\n                continue\n\n            flow = abs(flows[b_idx])\n            if cap > 0 and flow >= 0.99 * cap:\n                binding.append({\n                    'from': int(br[0]),\n                    'to': int(br[1]),\n                    'flow_MW': float(flow),\n                    'limit_MW': float(cap)\n                })\n\n        return binding\n\n    def find_line_target(self, from_bus, to_bus):\n        \"\"\"Find specific line\"\"\"\n        for idx, br in enumerate(self.branches):\n            f = int(br[0])\n            t = int(br[1])\n            if (f == from_bus and t == to_bus) or (f == to_bus and t == from_bus):\n                return idx, br[5]\n        return None, None\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network...\")\n    ma = MarketAnalysis('/root/network.json')\n\n    # CF line loading\n    cf_flow = abs(cf_flows[line_idx])\n    cf_loading = 100 * cf_flow / new_cap if new_cap else 0\n    cf_binding_flag = cf_loading >= 99\n    print(f\"Line 64-1501: {cf_flow:.1f}/{new_cap} MW ({cf_loading:.1f}%), binding={cf_binding_flag}\")\n\n    #
